SECOND STAGE (Spring 2024)

Promotion format:
- Top-2 teams of the spring stage are directly promoted to the First League.
- First (fall) stage champions play off the third team of second (spring) stage.
    If this is the same team, the playoff is canceled.
- If fall champions get direct promotion or relegation in spring, the second 
    fall team proceeds to the playoff.
- If second fall team also gets direct promotion or relegation in spring, 
    the third fall team proceed to the playoff.
- If all three top fall teams get direct promotion or relegation in spring, 
    the third spring team is automatically promoted to the First League.
